<script>
(function(){

const outputEl = document.getElementById("earningsOutput");
const productEl = document.getElementById("product");

let views = 1000000;
let commission = 0.20;

// Handle box clicks
function activateBox(parentId, value){
const box = document.getElementById(parentId);
box.querySelectorAll(".flaa-option").forEach(opt=>{
opt.classList.remove("active");
if(opt.dataset.value == value) opt.classList.add("active");
});
}

document.getElementById("viewsBox").addEventListener("click", e=>{
if(e.target.classList.contains("flaa-option")){
views = parseInt(e.target.dataset.value);
activateBox("viewsBox", views);
calc();
}
});

document.getElementById("commissionBox").addEventListener("click", e=>{
if(e.target.classList.contains("flaa-option")){
commission = parseFloat(e.target.dataset.value);
activateBox("commissionBox", commission);
calc();
}
});

productEl.addEventListener("change", calc);

function calc(){
const basePay = 1500;
const ctr = 0.005; // 0.5%
const lp = views * ctr;

let buyers = 0;
let price = 0;

if(productEl.value === "membership"){
buyers = lp * 0.015; // 1.5%
price = 50;
}
if(productEl.value === "coaching"){
buyers = lp * 0.0075; // 0.75%
price = 300;
}
if(productEl.value === "ht"){
buyers = lp * 0.0016; // 0.16%
price = 2000;
}

const creatorRevenue = buyers * price;
const yourCut = creatorRevenue * commission;
const total = Math.floor(yourCut + basePay);

animate(outputEl, total);
}

function animate(el, value){
const start = parseInt(el.innerText.replace(/\D/g,"")) || 0;
const diff = value - start;
const startTime = performance.now();
const duration = 800;

function tick(now){
const progress = Math.min((now - startTime)/duration, 1);
const current = Math.floor(start + diff * progress);
el.textContent = "$" + current.toLocaleString();
if(progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

calc();

})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
const el = document.getElementById('simple-universal-timer-a');
if (!el) return;

const pad2 = n => String(n|0).padStart(2,'0');
const fmt = sec => {
sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
const h = pad2(Math.floor(sec/3600));
const m = pad2(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60));
const s = pad2(sec%60);
return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
};
const render = sec => { el.textContent = fmt(sec); };

window.addEventListener('universalCountdownTick', e => {
render(e.detail.remainingSec);
});

const uc = window.UNIVERSAL_COUNTDOWN;
if (uc && Number.isFinite(uc.endMs)) {
render(Math.max(0, Math.floor((uc.endMs - Date.now())/1000)));
} else {
const stored = +localStorage.getItem('uc_end_ms');
if (stored > 0) render(Math.max(0, Math.floor((stored - Date.now())/1000)));
}
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
const pad2 = n => String(n|0).padStart(2,'0');
const fmt = sec => {
sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
const h = pad2(Math.floor(sec/3600));
const m = pad2(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60));
const s = pad2(sec%60);
return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
};
const clamp = (x,a,b)=>Math.min(b,Math.max(a,x));
const toInt = (v,f)=>{ const n=parseInt(v,10); return Number.isFinite(n)?n:f; };

document.querySelectorAll('.urgX').forEach(root=>{
const total = toInt(root.dataset.total,50);
const initClaimed = clamp(toInt(root.dataset.initialClaimed,0),0,total);
const durSec = toInt(root.dataset.duration,2580); // must match your global duration

const countEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-count');
const barEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-bar');
const claimedEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-claimed');
const leftEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-leftnum');
const actTxt = root.querySelector('.urgX-actTxt');

function renderFromRemaining(remainingSec){
// clamp remaining to [0, durSec] so progress stays 0..1 during the current cycle
const rem = clamp(remainingSec, 0, durSec);
const progress = 1 - (rem / durSec);

// claimed/left based on progress
const claimed = clamp(Math.round(initClaimed + (total - initClaimed) * progress), 0, total);
const left = Math.max(0, total - claimed);

if (claimedEl) claimedEl.textContent = `${claimed} claimed`;
if (leftEl) leftEl.textContent = `${left} left`;
if (barEl) barEl.style.width = ((claimed/total)*100).toFixed(1) + '%';
if (countEl) countEl.textContent = fmt(rem);

if (actTxt) {
if (left <= Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total*0.05))) actTxt.textContent = 'Almost gone…';
else if (left <= Math.ceil(total*0.2)) actTxt.textContent = 'Selling out quickly…';
else actTxt.textContent = 'Spots are selling fast…';
}
}

// Live: drive entirely from the universal tick
window.addEventListener('universalCountdownTick', e => {
renderFromRemaining(e.detail.remainingSec);
});

// Initial paint from current endMs (no intervals)
const uc = window.UNIVERSAL_COUNTDOWN;
let initialRem = 0;
if (uc && Number.isFinite(uc.endMs)) {
initialRem = Math.max(0, Math.floor((uc.endMs - Date.now())/1000));
} else {
const stored = +localStorage.getItem('uc_end_ms');
if (stored > 0) initialRem = Math.max(0, Math.floor((stored - Date.now())/1000));
}
renderFromRemaining(initialRem);
});
})();
</script>

<script>
const staticMessages = [
"✉️ Last Funnel System sold to mike*****@gmail.com",
"💬 “Made my money back in 3 days” – Chris P",
"📦 19 systems downloaded in the last 24h",
"🔒 100% secure checkout with 256-bit encryption",
];

const messageBox = document.getElementById("checkout-message-text");
const boxContainer = document.getElementById("checkout-message-box");

let msgIndex = 0;

function getRandomViewers() {
const viewers = 55 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 12); // 55–66
return `👁️ ${viewers} people viewing this right now`;
}

function rotateMessage() {
// Fade out
boxContainer.style.opacity = 0;

setTimeout(() => {
// Alternate between viewer stat and static message
let newMessage;
if (msgIndex % 2 === 0) {
newMessage = getRandomViewers();
} else {
newMessage = staticMessages[Math.floor(Math.random() * staticMessages.length)];
}

messageBox.textContent = newMessage;
boxContainer.style.opacity = 1;

msgIndex++;
}, 500); // Match CSS fade timing
}

// Initial start
setTimeout(rotateMessage, 4000);
setInterval(rotateMessage, 6000); // Every 6 seconds
</script>

<script>
(function(){
/* ---------- Banner animation cycle (unchanged visuals) ---------- */
const scrollingBanner = document.querySelector('.scrolling-banner');
const track = document.querySelector('.scrolling-track');
const discountBanner = document.getElementById('discountBanner');

const fadeDuration = 800;
const timeScrollingVisible = 15000;
const timeDiscountVisible = 10000;

function restartScroll(){
if(!track) return;
track.style.transform = "translateX(0)";
track.classList.remove('animate');
void track.offsetWidth;
track.classList.add('animate');
}

function cycle(){
restartScroll();
scrollingBanner?.classList.add('visible');
discountBanner?.classList.remove('visible');

setTimeout(()=>{
scrollingBanner?.classList.remove('visible');
setTimeout(()=>{
discountBanner?.classList.add('visible');
setTimeout(()=>{
discountBanner?.classList.remove('visible');
setTimeout(cycle, fadeDuration);
}, timeDiscountVisible);
}, fadeDuration);
}, timeScrollingVisible);
}
cycle();

/* ---------- Universal countdown hookup ---------- */
const countdownEl = document.getElementById('countdown');
if (!countdownEl) return;

const pad2 = n => String(n|0).padStart(2,'0');
const fmt = sec => {
sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
const h = pad2(Math.floor(sec/3600));
const m = pad2(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60));
const s = pad2(sec%60);
return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
};
const render = sec => { countdownEl.textContent = fmt(sec); };

// Live updates from the universal engine
window.addEventListener('universalCountdownTick', e => {
render(e.detail.remainingSec);
});

// Immediate paint from current endMs
const uc = window.UNIVERSAL_COUNTDOWN;
if (uc && Number.isFinite(uc.endMs)) {
const rem = Math.max(0, Math.floor((uc.endMs - Date.now())/1000));
render(rem);
} else {
const stored = +localStorage.getItem('uc_end_ms');
if (stored > 0) render(Math.max(0, Math.floor((stored - Date.now())/1000)));
}
})();
</script>

<script>
const names = [

"Caleb J.", "Dylan C.", "Jackson B.", "Owen L.", "Lucas V.", "Noah G.",

"Christian V.", "Dominic F.", "Evan J.", "Micah P.", "Tristan H.", "Luca B.",
"Jace N.", "Bryce M.", "Asher D.", "Isaac K.", "Gabriel R.", "Roman T.",
"Brody W.", "Grayson P.", "Carter L.", "Nolan H.", "Easton G.", "Maxwell J.",
"Levi S.", "Silas V.", "Maddox C.", "Ryder F.", "Enzo D.", "Jett K.",
"Malik A.", "Andre R.", "Omar T.", "Kobe N.", "Diego C.", "Santiago L.",
"Mateo R.", "Javier G.", "Emiliano V."
];

const cities = [
"Los Angeles, CA", "San Diego, CA", "San Francisco, CA", "Austin, TX",
"Houston, TX", "Dallas, TX", "Miami, FL", "Orlando, FL", "Tampa, FL",
"New York, NY", "Brooklyn, NY", "Queens, NY", "Seattle, WA", "Portland, OR",
"Denver, CO", "Boulder, CO", "Chicago, IL", "Nashville, TN", "Atlanta, GA",
"Phoenix, AZ", "Scottsdale, AZ", "Salt Lake City, UT", "Las Vegas, NV",
"Charlotte, NC", "Raleigh, NC", "Boston, MA", "Cambridge, MA"
];

const container = document.getElementById('notification-container');
const text1 = document.getElementById('notification-text-1');
const text2 = document.getElementById('notification-text-2');

function getRandomItem(arr) {
return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
}

function getRandomTime() {
const minutesAgo = Math.floor(Math.random() * 180) + 1;
if (minutesAgo < 60) {
return `${minutesAgo} minute${minutesAgo > 1 ? 's' : ''} ago`;
} else {
const hours = Math.floor(minutesAgo / 60);
return `${hours} hour${hours > 1 ? 's' : ''} ago`;
}
}

function showNotification() {
const name = getRandomItem(names);
const city = getRandomItem(cities);
const timeAgo = getRandomTime();

// Update the content
text1.innerHTML = `${name} launched their agency<br> in ${city}`;
text2.textContent = `${timeAgo}`;

// Show the container
container.classList.add('show');

// Hide after 5 seconds
setTimeout(() => {
container.classList.remove('show');
}, 5000);
}


// Show notification every 40 seconds
setInterval(showNotification, 40000);
</script>

<script>
(function(){
const el = document.getElementById('simple-universal-timer-b');
if (!el) return;

const pad2 = n => String(n|0).padStart(2,'0');
const fmt = sec => {
sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
const h = pad2(Math.floor(sec/3600));
const m = pad2(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60));
const s = pad2(sec%60);
return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
};
const render = sec => { el.textContent = fmt(sec); };

window.addEventListener('universalCountdownTick', e => {
render(e.detail.remainingSec);
});

const uc = window.UNIVERSAL_COUNTDOWN;
if (uc && Number.isFinite(uc.endMs)) {
render(Math.max(0, Math.floor((uc.endMs - Date.now())/1000)));
} else {
const stored = +localStorage.getItem('uc_end_ms');
if (stored > 0) render(Math.max(0, Math.floor((stored - Date.now())/1000)));
}
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
const pad2 = n => String(n|0).padStart(2,'0');
const fmt = sec => {
sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
const h = pad2(Math.floor(sec/3600));
const m = pad2(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60));
const s = pad2(sec%60);
return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
};
const clamp = (x,a,b)=>Math.min(b,Math.max(a,x));
const toInt = (v,f)=>{ const n=parseInt(v,10); return Number.isFinite(n)?n:f; };

document.querySelectorAll('.urgX').forEach(root=>{
const total = toInt(root.dataset.total,50);
const initClaimed = clamp(toInt(root.dataset.initialClaimed,0),0,total);
const durSec = toInt(root.dataset.duration,2580); // must match your global duration

const countEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-count');
const barEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-bar');
const claimedEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-claimed');
const leftEl = root.querySelector('.urgX-leftnum');
const actTxt = root.querySelector('.urgX-actTxt');

function renderFromRemaining(remainingSec){
// clamp remaining to [0, durSec] so progress stays 0..1 during the current cycle
const rem = clamp(remainingSec, 0, durSec);
const progress = 1 - (rem / durSec);

// claimed/left based on progress
const claimed = clamp(Math.round(initClaimed + (total - initClaimed) * progress), 0, total);
const left = Math.max(0, total - claimed);

if (claimedEl) claimedEl.textContent = `${claimed} claimed`;
if (leftEl) leftEl.textContent = `${left} left`;
if (barEl) barEl.style.width = ((claimed/total)*100).toFixed(1) + '%';
if (countEl) countEl.textContent = fmt(rem);

if (actTxt) {
if (left <= Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total*0.05))) actTxt.textContent = 'Almost gone…';
else if (left <= Math.ceil(total*0.2)) actTxt.textContent = 'Selling out quickly…';
else actTxt.textContent = 'Spots are selling fast…';
}
}

// Live: drive entirely from the universal tick
window.addEventListener('universalCountdownTick', e => {
renderFromRemaining(e.detail.remainingSec);
});

// Initial paint from current endMs (no intervals)
const uc = window.UNIVERSAL_COUNTDOWN;
let initialRem = 0;
if (uc && Number.isFinite(uc.endMs)) {
initialRem = Math.max(0, Math.floor((uc.endMs - Date.now())/1000));
} else {
const stored = +localStorage.getItem('uc_end_ms');
if (stored > 0) initialRem = Math.max(0, Math.floor((stored - Date.now())/1000));
}
renderFromRemaining(initialRem);
});
})();
</script>

<!-- JavaScript -->
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
const carouselWrapper = document.getElementById("carouselWrapper");
const slides = document.querySelectorAll(".review-card");
const progressSlice = document.querySelector(".progress-slice");
const prevArrow = document.getElementById("prevArrow");
const nextArrow = document.getElementById("nextArrow");
const progressBar = document.querySelector(".carousel-progress");

const totalSlides = slides.length;
let currentIndex = 0;

const slideWidth = slides[0].offsetWidth + 40; // Slide width + gap

let startX = 0;
let isDragging = false;
let autoScroll;

/** Update Carousel */
function updateCarousel() {
const offset = -currentIndex * slideWidth;
carouselWrapper.style.transition = "transform 0.3s ease-in-out";
carouselWrapper.style.transform = `translateX(${offset}px)`;
updateProgress();
}

/** Update Progress Bar */
function updateProgress() {
const progressBarWidth = progressBar.offsetWidth; // Actual width of progress bar
const stepSize = progressBarWidth / totalSlides; // Width of each "step"
const position = currentIndex * stepSize; // Calculate position based on current slide
progressSlice.style.transform = `translateX(${position}px)`;
}

/** Reset Auto-Scroll */
function resetAutoScroll() {
clearInterval(autoScroll);
autoScroll = setInterval(() => {
if (currentIndex < totalSlides - 1) {
currentIndex++;
} else {
currentIndex = 0;
}
updateCarousel();
}, 4500);
}

/** Arrow Clicks */
prevArrow.addEventListener("click", () => {
if (currentIndex > 0) {
currentIndex--;
updateCarousel();
resetAutoScroll();
}
});

nextArrow.addEventListener("click", () => {
if (currentIndex < totalSlides - 1) {
currentIndex++;
} else {
currentIndex = 0; // Loop back to the first slide
}
updateCarousel();
resetAutoScroll();
});

/** Touch Swipe */
carouselWrapper.addEventListener("touchstart", (e) => {
isDragging = true;
startX = e.touches[0].clientX; // Record starting touch point
carouselWrapper.style.transition = "none";
clearInterval(autoScroll); // Pause auto-scroll on swipe
});

carouselWrapper.addEventListener("touchmove", (e) => {
if (!isDragging) return;
const currentX = e.touches[0].clientX;
const moveX = currentX - startX;
const offset = -currentIndex * slideWidth + moveX;
carouselWrapper.style.transform = `translateX(${offset}px)`;
});

carouselWrapper.addEventListener("touchend", (e) => {
isDragging = false;
const endX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
const diffX = startX - endX;

if (diffX > 50 && currentIndex < totalSlides - 1) {
// Swipe left
currentIndex++;
} else if (diffX < -50 && currentIndex > 0) {
// Swipe right
currentIndex--;
}
updateCarousel();
resetAutoScroll();
});

/** Initialize */
window.addEventListener("resize", updateProgress); // Update progress bar on resize
resetAutoScroll();
updateCarousel();
});
</script></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!
